HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000703/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_12e833af3fd76cf4478a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:20:2\",\"root_000025\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:20:4\",\"root_000703\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yer, gök ve dağlarla birlikte kurulan üst-orta-alt düzeninin yaşanabilir, uzanmış çalışma düzlemidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000025/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_274f81fe247052044c5f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:20:2\",\"root_000025\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yerde oyalanıp kalma ihtimali, sonradan bildirilen ilahi dönüşle düzeltilir: Yer üzerinde yürünüp incelenen bir yüzeydir, nihai varış noktası değildir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_000703/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3e180b4ea09e6eed18f5","text":"2. **Fertile ground beneath the pastoral signs.** Evidence: `ء ر ض` B002 soft fertile ground; `س ط ح` B006 plant spreading along ground; earlier `ض ر ع` plant, `ن ع م` livestock, `ء ب ل` camels. Structural cue: food/pasture and camel imagery culminate in earth’s surface. Role: the spread earth is the base for pasture and provision. Abductive move: nourishment depends on the relation between water, animal, plant, and ground. Reading change: earth’s flattening becomes ecological preparation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B002","root_000703/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3f178bade31811f07d08","text":"3. **Flattening as reversal of bodies laid low.** Evidence: `س ط ح` B002 body laid flat on ground; `خ ش ع` B002 low ground; earlier faces humbled. Structural cue: punished faces are lowered; earth is spread. Role: the same flatness can be ordered ground or defeated body. Abductive move: moral reading distinguishes beneficial leveling from humiliating collapse. Reading change: `سطحت` is not simply flattening; it is purposeful surface-making.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000703/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_41e9c30666a052cd82f9","text":"1. **Earth as lower plane spread for use.** Evidence: `ء ر ض` B001 lower ground opposite sky; `س ط ح` B001 flat extended surface. `ك ي ف` has no branch inventory. Structural cue: the inspection sequence descends from camel to sky, mountains, earth; this line completes the spatial frame. Role: earth is the usable lower surface, not inert backdrop. Abductive move: the created world is readable as arranged planes and supports. Reading change: the ayah reads as attention to habitability and extension.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_000703/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5cfffd7f82cdfdfcb727","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:20:2\",\"root_000025\",[\"B002\"]],[\"88:20:4\",\"root_000703\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yumuşak verimli toprak ve yerde yayılan bitki çağrışımları, düzleştirmeyi su, otlak, hayvan ve beslenme ilişkisine hazırlanmış ekolojik bir taban yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B005","root_000703/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_73c69110c0ff7d7b6c46","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:20:2\",\"root_000025\",[\"B005\"]],[\"88:20:4\",\"root_000703\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kalın yaygı ve çalışma hasırı çağrışımları, serilmiş halıları dünya ölçeğine çıkararak yeri oturma ve eylem için hazırlanmış büyük bir destek gibi gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000703/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_80a68fcfded01de7db74","text":"3. **A tent-like architectural model links support, cover, and floor.** **Lexical:** `س ط ح` B003, \"a support that stretches out a tent,\" alongside B001; `ء ر ض` B001 supplies the floor. **Cue:** the sky is raised as overhead cover, mountains stand upright, and earth is extended beneath them in 88:18-20. **Role:** the support branch assigns the upright middle forms a possible stretching function between cover and floor. **Abduction:** I infer a tent analogy; the packet gives no claim that mountains literally hold the sky. **Reading change:** `سطحت` participates in a coherent architectural image: an extended floor beneath a raised covering articulated by standing supports.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000703/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9712ff5ed12d3d744ddc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:20:4\",\"root_000703\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yere serilmiş beden çağrışımı, önceki alçaltılmış yüzlerin aksine burada düzlüğün yenilgi değil, yararlı bir yüzey kurma işi olduğunu gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b88413022e2b016d9da2","text":"The garden carpets in 88:16 had already prepared a small-scale version of spread surface, so the earth appeared as cosmic furnishing. The bodily branch of `س ط ح` also sent a shadow back to the humbled faces.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bc3b19f6a81c7e21ec9e","text":"88:21 turned the surfaced earth from a passive object into evidence used by reminder. The strongest later surprise was 88:25's `إِلَيْنَا`: it folds the earlier outward `إِلَى ٱلْأَرْضِ` back toward the observer's own return, correcting the earth from final destination to traversed surface.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bfe5df9044cd3e97f45e","text":"2. **The spread carpets activate a textile analogy for the surfaced earth.** **Lexical:** `ء ر ض` B005, \"the thick mat,\" and `س ط ح` B005, \"a flat working place or mat,\" activated with B001. **Cue:** `زَرَابِىُّ مَبْثُوثَةٌ` in 88:16 precedes the world-observation sequence, while 88:20 uses passive surfacing. **Role:** the carpet supplies a familiar prepared surface; earth and `سطح` branches enlarge that material logic to the inhabited world. **Abduction:** I infer that the earlier furnishing is an analogy rather than merely nearby decoration. **Reading change:** the earth becomes a vast laid-out support, prepared for dwelling and action as a carpeted place is prepared for use.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000025/B006","root_000703/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e636b1ff0125c0530224","text":"4. **The ground is not the final destination of directed movement.** **Lexical:** `ء ر ض` B001, lower ground, and B006, sticking to the ground and lingering; `س ط ح` B001 supplies extension. **Cue:** the gaze is directed `إِلَى ٱلْأَرْضِ`, but 88:25 later redirects the people themselves `إِلَيْنَا` in return. **Role:** the earth receives sight and bodily movement as a surface, while the return statement prevents it from becoming the terminal point. **Abduction:** I infer a correction from repeated directional syntax. **Reading change:** the surfaced earth is a traversable and inspectable plane, not an ultimate place in which persons can simply remain.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000703/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_efdfbaafe377745ad6e8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:20:4\",\"root_000703\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Çadırı geren destek çağrışımı, göğü örtü, dağları dikili ara parçalar ve yeri taban olarak birleştiren mimari bir benzetme önerir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f384c12f15df35c93474","text":"1. **The earth is the lower, usable plane in a relational construction.** **Lexical:** `ء ر ض` B001, \"the lower ground opposite the sky,\" and `س ط ح` B001, \"a flat extended surface.\" **Cue:** raised sky in 88:18, upright mountains in 88:19, and surfaced earth in 88:20 form a repeated passive `كَيْفَ` series. **Role:** earth supplies the lower counterpart, surfacing supplies extension, and the neighboring objects establish its vertical and interruptive relations. **Abduction:** I infer that habitability emerges from these relations, not from flatness alone. **Reading change:** the earth reads as a deliberately coordinated working plane within an upper-middle-lower system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies
